description: Just steps from the sands of Gulf Shores Public Beach, The Catch Seafood Grill and Bar is where amazing food, unforgettable cocktails, good company, and the sound of the Gulf come together. Whether you're here for a beach day bite, dinner with friends, or a relaxing cocktail at the bar or on our patio during sunset, we're ready to serve something memorable. Located right across from Gulf Shores Public Beach and just minutes from Orange Beach and Foley, we're easy to find and even easier to love. At The Catch, we believe great seafood starts right here at home. Our menu is built around the freshest wild-caught Gulf shrimp and seafood, paired with the bold, comforting flavors the Gulf Coast is known for. Our signature seafood boils are a true Gulf feast piled high with crab legs, Gulf shrimp, mussels, Conecuh sausage, corn on the cob, and red potatoes, all tossed in our bold, house-made garlic butter sauces you won't find anywhere else. Craving something crispy? Try our fried shrimp or fried fish platters, served golden with hushpuppies, creamy coleslaw, and fried okra. Looking for something a little indulgent? Don't miss our Garlic Butter Spiny Lobster or grilled salmon plates, both served with mashed potatoes and fresh grilled veggies. Or dig into guest favorites like the Bayou Crawfish Pasta or our Southern-style crab cakes, packed with Gulf flavor and made to be remembered. Our full bar features a wide selection of signature cocktails, frozen daiquiris, and local favorites perfect for sipping under the sun or catching golden hour by the water. Every drink is crafted with you in mind from bold, refreshing flavors to classic favorites that complement your beach day, lunch, or sunset dinner. Gulf Shores is known for family vacations, reunions, bridal trips, and sporting celebrations, and we've made sure there's room for everyone at The Catch. Stop by solo, bring a meeting of friends, or gather the whole team we're built for every kind of celebration. With spacious indoor dining, a lively full bar area, and breezy patio seating perfect for larger groups, The Catch is the ultimate stop for family meals, group celebrations, or a casual bite after a day at the beach.
